To address this gap, we introduce BANGLAWILD, a benchmark of 2,535 Bengali scene text images, each paired with a verbatim gold transcription, two categorical axes, four diagnostic attributes, and an orthographically standard form where the in-image text deviates from canonical spelling. We evaluate fifteen VLMs and three conventional OCR systems under three prompting strategies, fine-tune 6 open-source models with LoRA, and complement edit-distance metrics with an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our results reveal a persistent gap in which larger models within the same family do not outperform smaller ones. Our fifteen-class error taxonomy shows that visual mis-recognition accounts for ~60% of errors in the strongest systems, while conjunct-related errors contribute under 2%, challenging a long-standing assumption in Bengali OCR research; the same visual dominant profile also holds across architectures, including the one conventional baseline that reads Bengali reliably. Prompt language mainly affects cross-script drift and LoRA reduces catastrophic failures in weak models without lifting the ceiling on already competent ones. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [101] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuRA提出多秩自适应的测试时适配框架，根据token级视觉复杂度动态选择和融合不同容量的低秩适配模块，解决固定秩在分布偏移下欠/过拟合的瓶颈，并配以理论证明与SOTA实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法采用静态低秩配置，而视觉输入信息密度天然可变，固定秩导致复杂场景欠拟合、简单场景过拟合，成为参数高效TTA的根本瓶颈。

Method: (1) Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition：提供知识保持的初始化；(2) Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating：依据token级视觉复杂度学习语义到秩的映射；(3) 数学上证明自适应机制的必要性与梯度稳定性；(4) 动态设计在最深视觉层生效利用最短梯度回传路径。

Result: 在广泛的域泛化与跨数据集基准上达到SOTA准确率，同时显著降低计算和内存开销。

Conclusion: 动态多秩适配是解决测试时视觉分布偏移的有效范式，理论完备且工程效率高。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [102] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个胸部X光视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过将辅助判别监督(分类和定位头)与奖励对齐生成(DAPO策略优化)统一起来，在报告生成、VQA和空间定位上取得SOTA性能。同时耦合Qwen3-VL-4B与工具调用实现测量依赖诊断，提升F1达43.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的胸部X光系统将报告生成、发现分类、空间定位和解剖测量视为分离问题，导致生成式VLM与放射科医生的实际需求之间存在差距。需要一个统一框架同时具备可调决策阈值的分类能力、空间定位能力、解剖测量能力以及高质量的流畅报告生成。

Method: 1) CARE-X在生成骨干上增加focal-loss分类头和composite-loss接地(grounding)头，与语言建模目标联合训练；2) 提出Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO)利用任务特定奖励信号优化报告生成、VQA和空间接地；3) 耦合Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct的原生工具调用能力，在保留完整图像视觉访问的同时调用确定性测量工具。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准上大部分指标达到SOTA；ReXVQA VQA准确率94.0%(比次优基线高6.0个百分点)；生成式空间解码接近专用检测头的性能；测量依赖条件上平均F1比仅感知基线提升43.6个百分点(5种测量依赖条件)。

Conclusion: 结构化判别预测与生成任务可以相互增强，统一的判别+生成架构加上奖励对齐优化和工具调用的混合推理，能够显著提升胸部X光分析系统的临床实用性。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [103] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NCGR模块，通过预测二维矫正偏移和相机级门控来补偿自动驾驶BEV检测中外参扰动导致的投影误差，无需显式估计六自由度外参矫正，在动态外参扰动下显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于空间交叉注意力（SCA）的BEV 3D检测器假设外参准确且固定，但实际中外参扰动会使BEV参考点在图像平面的投影偏移，导致query采样错误区域，检测性能严重下降。

Method: 对每个query-camera对预测2D矫正偏移，通过相机级门控调制后矫正基础投影，再进行本地可变形采样。训练时利用扰动导出量构建条件和门控，并通过调度插值逐步替换为由辅助标量预测的对应物，实现无扰动元数据的盲推断。使用权重共享的干净教师/扰动学生配对，通过BEV一致性目标监督矫正模块。

Result: 在nuScenes五相机动态压力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，优于BEVFormer的28.00%和CAPE的33.23%；在干净外参下，性能与BEVFormer相当。

Conclusion: NCGR有效补偿了外参扰动带来的投影误差，显著提升BEV检测的鲁棒性，且不依赖完整的六自由度外参估计，在干净环境下也保持原有性能。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [104] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了UniEvo-RS，一个统一的遥感分割框架，通过多指令提示数据集统一文本/视觉驱动提示，并引入基于代表性示例反馈驱动的免训练原型进化机制，在批量标注中实现对不可见类别的渐进式精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态视觉语言模型在遥感新场景、新类别和视觉混淆背景下性能退化严重；现有统一范式缺乏灵活的任务路由来适应多意图操作流程；实际批量制图中标注者通常先用少量代表性样本精修再进行大规模处理。

Method: 1）构建多指令提示数据集，在单一架构中统一文本驱动和视觉驱动提示，建立动态任务路由机制；2）引入代表性反馈驱动的免训练原型进化机制，通过对比示例上的手动标注与初始预测，将预测误差蒸馏为正/负原型，增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声，采用固定预算聚类内存。

Result: 实验表明UniEvo-RS在大多数设置下取得最先进性能；在少量示例上进行最小交互后，能够在批量标注期间对不可见类别实现免训练的渐进式精度提升。

Conclusion: 证明通过在少量代表性示例上的交互式反馈，可以驱动原型进化实现免训练的持续性能改进，为遥感批量标注提供了实用且高效的统一框架。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [105] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于流式视频目标分割（VOS）的跟踪器，通过将记忆机制重新设计为在模型内优化并以帧率运行，解决了离线高精度跟踪器在实时30fps流式协议下性能崩溃的问题。方法引入单一学习到的存在信号来管理记忆的写入、读取范围、输出抑制和重新检测，实验证明在四个基准和五个基线上成为最强流式跟踪器，并在最难内容上超过其构建来源的离线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS跟踪器（如DAM4SAM）在离线逐帧评测中精度领先，但在严格的30fps流式协议下（错过预算的帧返回上一帧掩码），其高精度依赖的丰富记忆管线过于缓慢无法跟上帧率，且输出无法判断目标是否仍存在。作者将两个失败根源都追溯到跟踪器的记忆管线，并针对流式场景重建该管线。

Method: 方法核心是在模型内部进行优化（in-model optimization），使记忆机制本身以帧率运行，而非使用事后补救的降级方案。通过一个单一的学习性存在信号（learned presence signal）统一控制四个决策：记忆条目如何进入记忆、跟踪器回溯多远的记忆范围、何时抑制输出、何时重新检测目标。机制分析表明固定策略无法胜任——因为目标真正消失时有效的控制策略，在目标只是难以看清时反而有害，因此必须逐帧决策。

Result: 在四个基准和五个现代基线上，方法成为最强的流式跟踪器，在时钟约束下几乎恢复离线模型的全部精度，并且在最难的内容上超过了其构建来源的离线模型。

Conclusion: 结论是流式VOS的精度瓶颈不在模型本身，而在记忆管线的设计；通过让记忆控制以可学习的逐帧感知信号驱动、并将优化嵌入模型内部，可以同时实现实时性和高精度，甚至超越其离线源头模型。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [106] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoFrame是一个无需训练的框架，用于长视频理解中的查询自适应视觉证据调度。它利用VLM的推理反馈（熵门控预算调度+注意力引导候选提案）来决定何时增加帧预算以及在哪里搜索额外证据，在多个基准上实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视频理解方法要么依赖静态一次性选择（固定帧预算和候选池），要么使用昂贵的多轮推理的agent调度器。目标是实现低开销的查询自适应视觉证据调度，既高效又有自适应性。

Method: EcoFrame包含两个核心组件：(1) 熵门控预算调度——使用输出不确定性来决定是提前停止（证据足够）还是逐步扩大帧预算；(2) 注意力引导候选提案——将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息密集区域进行密集局部搜索，同时当注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU上，EcoFrame在多个VLM骨干上实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-VL上平均准确率64.4，超过BOLT的63.5，相比AKS和BOLT提供1.85倍加速；与agent-based的A.I.R.相比，保持相当精度的同时最高13.5倍推理加速。

Conclusion: EcoFrame通过利用VLM自身推理反馈实现训练-free的查询自适应帧调度，在精度和效率之间取得了更好的平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [107] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP)，识别归一化仿射参数为低维高杠杆子空间，通过仅微调这些参数来优化低比特量化鲁棒性。在PTQ中冻结主干权重仅微调仿射参数以提升量化友好性；在QAT中引入交替训练模式解耦特征学习与数值校准，打破联合训练的饱和上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上精度严重退化，根本原因在于全参数耦合训练范式忽略了参数子空间的异质性。紧凑网络特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差，而现有PTQ和QAT方法均未能针对性地解决这一问题。

Method: 理论分析证明BN仿射参数可完全抵消量化失真的通道级仿射分量，非线性舍入和裁剪残差构成不可约误差边界。在此基础上提出NAP：PTQ场景中冻结主干权重仅微调仿射参数（在目标伪量化图上）；QAT场景中采用交替QAT-NAP策略，解耦特征学习与数值校准；蒸馏引导的NAP作为方向性平坦度优化，将师生logit失配投影到受限子空间。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上的实验表明：NAP能恢复严重崩塌的低比特量化，持续提升基于重建的PTQ方法，并以极低调优成本超越饱和的全参数QAT。

Conclusion: 揭示了定向低维子空间优化的原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [108] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAR提出了一种基于码本的人脸修复框架，通过双输入特征提取获取组件级几何描述，采用Aligned Geometric Priors Injector（KV-Q交换策略）生成几何对齐特征，并将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）的粗到细生成过程，在合成和三个真实世界基准上取得竞争性感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有码本式盲人脸修复（BFR）方法在严重退化条件下存在两个关键问题：一是条件特征模糊不清（ambiguity），缺乏明确的几何锚点；二是预测机制脆弱，单步映射难以在严重退化下生成精细面部结构。作者旨在通过几何对齐特征和逐步MAR精化来解决这两个根本缺陷。

Method: GeoMAR包含两大核心模块：(1) 双输入提取管线：提取基于组件（component-based）的几何描述，带有显式、空间保真的锚点，生成文本级先验；(2) Aligned Geometric Priors Injector：通过KV-Q（Key-Value到Query）交换策略将文本先验与低质量（LQ）特征集成，产出几何对齐特征；(3) 多步掩码自回归（MAR）精化：将传统的一步映射改造成多步MAR过程，以粗到细的生成方式逐步精化复杂面部区域。

Result: 在一个合成基准和三个真实世界基准上的实验表明，GeoMAR在感知质量和视觉结构连贯性方面达到高度竞争力，相比现有方法取得更好的修复效果。

Conclusion: GeoMAR证明了几何对齐特征注入与掩码自回归逐步精化相结合能够有效解决码本式盲人脸修复中的条件模糊和预测脆弱问题，为该方向提供了一种新的框架范式。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [109] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的指纹重叠分离方法，将重叠指纹分离视为图像修复任务，从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发，分阶段加入指纹先验、部分指纹补全能力，最终通过多通道条件化的重叠感知修复（overlap-aware inpainting）重建每个分量指纹。在两个公开数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊线模式是刑侦现场潜在指纹恢复和活体扫描场景中的常见难题，现有方法要么依赖基于规则的取向场补全（需要强领域知识），要么训练端到端深度网络（未考虑领域特定知识），两者均有局限。

Method: 将分离问题形式化为图像修复任务，采用多阶段渐进式训练：1）从预训练Stable Diffusion起步；2）逐步引入指纹先验；3）加入部分指纹补全能力；4）提出overlap-aware inpainting，基于多通道条件化，用扩散修复模型分别重建每个分量指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，通过所提出的基于扩散修复的方法重建的分量指纹能够以非常高的概率与其配对指纹匹配成功。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的overlap-aware inpainting能够有效分离重叠指纹，充分利用了Stable Diffusion的先验知识和领域特定的指纹信息，相比现有方法在匹配准确率上表现优越。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [110] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-Design 是一个以语义 RGBA 图层为原子单位的图像生成与编辑框架，包含 T2RGBA（文本生成独立 RGBA 资产）和 I2L（图像分解为完整语义图层）两个模型。I2L 在 Crello 基准上将每层 RGB L1 误差降低 37%，Alpha Soft IoU 相对提升 34%；T2RGBA 在 CLIP Score 上超越 LayerDiffuse 和 OmniAlpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成以像素为单位，缺乏对图像"如何被创建、理解和编辑"的结构化表示。借鉴人类设计师以图层而非原始像素进行创作的方式，作者希望为多模态生成模型提供图层原生的设计空间，使图像生成与编辑更接近专业设计工作流。

Method: 框架包含两个模型：(1) T2RGBA 模型直接由文本生成独立 RGBA 资产；(2) I2L 模型以已完成图像、全局指令和逐层提示为条件，联合生成有序、完整的语义 RGBA 图层。指令接口支持顶层分解、递归分解和目标提取，使分层成为可寻址的智能化编辑操作。I2L 学习完整语义对象而非可见像素分割，因此图层在移动或移除后仍可使用。

Result: 在 Crello 基准上，I2L 将每层 RGB L1 误差降低 37%，Alpha Soft IoU 相对提升 34%（优于 Qwen-Image-Layered）；T2RGBA 在 CLIP Score 上达到最高，超越 LayerDiffuse 和 OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: 以图层为原子单位的生成框架能够同时支持图像生成、理解与编辑，且语义完整的图层表示在编辑场景中保持可用性，为智能体驱动的图像编辑提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [111] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit是一个16B参数的流式自回归扩散框架，用于实时视频编辑。它通过分块自回归适配、Source-Anchored分布匹配蒸馏(SA-DMD)和长时程自回归蒸馏，在单张Nvidia B200 GPU上实现约30 FPS的720p端到端实时视频编辑，优于现有流式编辑系统并与强离线系统竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟的因果生成、有界计算资源，同时保持源保真度和长期时间一致性。现有方法要么依赖未来帧或预定义视频时长，要么在推理速度、源保真度和时间一致性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 分块自回归适配(chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation)：按块逐步生成，不依赖未来帧；2) Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD)：在两步生成中保持源保真度，减少训练-推理不匹配；3) Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation：缓解长时间生成中的累积时间漂移。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，JoyAI-Video-Edit显著优于现有流式编辑系统，在短视频和长视频上均与强离线系统保持竞争力。完整系统在单张Nvidia B200 GPU上实现720p约30 FPS的端到端实时视频编辑。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可在单GPU上实现实时、开放时长视频编辑的自回归扩散框架，通过三种蒸馏/适配策略有效解决了流式生成中的源保真度和长期时间一致性问题，并开源代码。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [112] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR)，将多模态智能体从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决视频场景下智能体的模态偏见和参数知识泄漏问题。框架采用解耦的感知-探索流水线，通过两阶段训练（SFT + GRPO）实现自主探索。同时构建了Video-DR-Bench基准（200个多跳VQA实例），在35B-A3B规模上达到64.0%平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet等专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态智能体面临两个关键瓶颈：(1) 模态偏见——智能体倾向于使用文本搜索而非视觉工具；(2) 参数知识泄漏——模型依赖内部记忆而非真正的工具增强执行。这两个问题阻碍了智能体在处理连续视频流时的可靠性和可信度，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: Video-DR采用解耦的感知-探索流水线，包含阶段式工具解锁机制，强制模型在web检索之前完成穷尽的跨帧视觉grounding。训练采用两阶段方案：先进行监督微调（SFT），再使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行强化学习，以突破模仿学习上限实现自主探索。同时构建了人类-AI协作的Video-DR-Bench基准（200个多跳VQA实例）。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B达到64.0%平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) 5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5 (52.5%)和Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%)。30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet竞争力相当，展示了紧凑规模下训练范式的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了模态偏见和参数知识泄漏是视频智能体需要解决的核心问题，解耦的感知-探索流水线配合GRPO训练能够有效克服这些瓶颈。即使在紧凑规模下也能达到与顶级专有模型竞争的性能，为视频级多模态智能体研究提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [113] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于免训练开放词汇语义分割的即插即用方法PTC（原型引导文本校准），通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入，缩小通用类别概念与具体实例视觉表征之间的语义差距，无需额外训练或外部模型，在八个基准上显著提升六种现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练开放词汇语义分割方法通常只改进视觉表征，而文本嵌入仅编码通用类别概念作为固定分类参考，导致通用概念与捕捉目标实例具体外观的视觉表征之间存在语义鸿沟，产生不完整掩膜和错误预测。受知觉锚定中符号-知觉对应的启发，作者希望校准文本嵌入以对齐实例级视觉表征。

Method: PTC包含两个阶段：(1) 感知阶段（Perceiving）：基于初始匹配分数选择可靠视觉证据，构建类别特定的视觉原型；(2) 锚定阶段（Anchoring）：使用这些原型校准对应文本嵌入，校准强度根据视觉证据量自适应调整。校准后的文本嵌入更准确地对齐实例级视觉表征，同时保留通用类别语义和开放词汇泛化能力。该方法无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为即插即用模块。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性分割方法的性能，产生更完整、更准确的分割结果。

Conclusion: PTC是一种简单有效的提升视觉-文本对齐的即插即用方法，通过原型引导的文本校准有效缓解了通用类别概念与实例级视觉表征之间的语义差距问题。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


### [114] [ParVL: Parallel Scaling and Expandable Compute Allocation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04010)
*Yang Yang,Qinyu Zhao,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaohui Li,Lixin Gu,Wenhai Wang,Hongjie Zhang,Wenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParVL提出了一种面向多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的并行缩放框架，通过复用已有的ViT和LLM骨干参数，在多个视觉和语言分支上分配并行计算，以固定骨干参数预算为约束，系统研究视觉与语言模态之间的计算分配权衡，并在约13B token上全参数微调训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM缩放策略要么扩展模型参数，要么扩展顺序推理计算，导致大量内存或延迟开销；更重要的是，现有方法无法改变ViT与LLM组件之间固定的计算分配，限制了任务特定优化。作者旨在解决这一刚性问题。

Method: 引入Parallel Vision-Language (ParVL)缩放框架：复用现有ViT和LLM骨干参数，通过分支特定的prefix参数在共享骨干上实例化多个并行计算流；使用约13B tokens进行全参数端到端监督微调；系统研究视觉编码器与LLM解码器之间的计算分配权衡。

Result: ParVL在多种多模态任务上优于同配方单分支基线；最优的视觉-语言计算分配因任务而异，表明任务特定优化确实可行。

Conclusion: 通过并行共享骨干计算而非扩展参数或顺序推理，ParVL能够在不增加显著内存/延迟开销的情况下提升MLLM性能，且视觉-语言计算分配是可调节、任务相关的。

Abstract: Existing scaling strategies for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically expand either model parameters or sequential inference computation, incurring substantial memory or latency overhead. More importantly, most existing methods fail to alter the rigid, fixed computation allocation between the Vision Transformer and the Large Language Model components, limiting task-specific optimization. To address this, we introduce the Parallel Vision-Language (ParVL) scaling framework for MLLMs, which scales parallel computation by reusing the existing ViT and LLM backbone parameters across multiple vision and language branches. This framework raises a central question: given a fixed backbone parameter budget, how should additional shared-backbone computation be allocated between the vision and language modalities? We instantiate each parallel computational stream with branch-specific prefix parameters over a shared backbone, and train the entire model end-to-end via full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on roughly 13B tokens. We systematically study the computation-allocation trade-off between the ViT encoder and LLM decoder. ParVL improves overall multimodal performance over same-recipe single-branch baselines, and the best evaluated vision--language allocation varies across tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YangYangGirl/ParVL.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Control Barrier Functions via Minkowski Operations for Safe Navigation among Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02886)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Calin Belta,Michael Otte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多面体机器人-多面体障碍物的精确有符号距离场（SDF）公式，并将其与非光滑控制屏障函数（CBF）集成，通过Minkowski运算和两个伴随凸规划计算精确SDF，并利用灵敏度分析推导出统一的解析梯度表达式，用于非保守的安全导航控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CBF的安全控制方法通常将多面体边界近似为球或椭球等保守光滑形状，导致可行空间被过度收缩，无法充分利用精确几何信息进行导航。需要一种能够处理精确多面体几何且保持可微性的SDF-CBF框架。

Method: 利用Minkowski和运算，将多面体机器人-多面体障碍物间的精确SDF建模为两个伴随凸规划在碰撞-free（正号）和碰撞（负号）情况下的解；利用2D Minkowski运算的几何性质和凸规划最优性条件，通过灵敏度分析推导出精确SDF梯度的统一解析表达式；将精确SDF与非光滑CBF集成，形成安全控制策略。

Result: 在纯平移场景和多个非完整单轮车模型（包括不安全初始状态恢复、单障碍和多障碍规避）上验证了框架的有效性。与基线方法对比显示，该框架支持非保守机动和安全性恢复，并揭示了由几何与非完整运动学耦合导致的先前被掩盖的局部最小值。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种不损失精确性的几何感知安全控制方案，为CBF方法在精确多面体环境中的实际应用提供了理论基础和可计算框架，同时揭示了精确几何建模对揭示系统动力学陷阱的重要性。

Abstract: Safely navigating polytopic environments while respecting the dynamics, control, and exact geometry of the underlying system is a challenge in robotics. Control barrier functions (CBFs) synthesize safe control policies by rendering the safe set forward invariant, but many existing CBF-based methods approximate polytopes using conservative smooth shapes, such as spheres or ellipsoids, to obtain explicit differentiable distance functions. In this article, we propose an exact Signed Distance Function (SDF) formulation for a {\it polytopic} robot and {\it polytopic} obstacles and integrate it with nonsmooth CBFs. Leveraging Minkowski operations, the proposed method computes the exact SDF via companion convex programs in both the collision-free (positive-sign) and in-collision (negative-sign) cases. Furthermore, by exploiting the convenient geometric properties of 2D Minkowski operations and the optimality conditions of the two companion convex programs, we derive a unified analytical expression for the gradient of the exact SDF via sensitivity analysis. The exact rotational gradient further reveals a previously masked class of local minima induced by the coupling between geometry and nonholonomic kinematics. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework through a pure-translation case and three scenarios with unicycle models involving recovery from an unsafe initialization and single- and multiple-obstacle avoidance. Comparisons with baseline methods highlight how the proposed framework enables non-conservative maneuvers and safety recovery.

</details>


### [116] [EmbodiedVAE: Disentangled Video VAE for Efficient and Controllable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02990)
*Jiayi Luo,Hanxin Zhu,Chen Gao,Jiankun Wang,Cong Wang,Tianyu He,Jianxin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedVAE是一种为机器人操作世界模型设计的视频VAE，采用双编码器单解码器架构和非对称时空压缩模块，解耦机械臂运动与环境背景，并提供紧凑但可控的隐空间表示，同时引入基于最优传输的一致性模块保证运动时序一致性。在机器人操作场景中相比SOTA视频VAE平均提升2dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDM依赖为自然场景优化的VAE，未考虑具身操作场景的特殊性，导致隐表示既不紧凑也不可控，阻碍了LDM的高效训练和机器人的精确控制。

Method: 采用双编码器-单解码器架构，通过非对称时空压缩模块自动解耦机械臂运动与环境背景；引入基于最优传输的一致性模块，显式强化运动保真度和帧间一致性。

Result: 在机器人操作场景中，相较于SOTA视频VAE平均提升2dB PSNR，重建质量和高压缩率俱佳，同时实现更精确的动作控制。

Conclusion: EmbodiedVAE证明了面向具身操作场景定制视频VAE的价值，通过解耦运动和背景并保持时序一致性，为机器人操作世界模型提供了更高效且可控的隐空间表示。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) have recently significantly advanced embodied learning in constructing powerful embodied manipulation world models. However, despite the remarkable performance, existing LDMs predominantly rely on Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) optimized for natural scenes while failing to account for the unique characteristics of embodied manipulation scenarios, yielding latent representations that are neither compact nor controllable, thereby hindering efficient training of LDMs and precise robotic control. To solve this problem, we present EmbodiedVAE, a novel video VAE that provides compact yet controllable latent representations tailored for the robotic manipulation world models. Specifically, EmbodiedVAE adopts a dual-encoder, single-decoder architecture with an asymmetric spatio-temporal compression module, which automatically disentangles the robot arm's motion from background environment, resulting in overall compactness while providing explicit embodied latent to support fine-grained action control. To further preserve the temporal consistency of learned robotic motion latent, we introduce an optimal-transport-based consistency module that explicitly enforces motion fidelity and inter-frame coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed EmbodiedVAE achieves superior reconstruction quality with high compression rate, while enabling more precise action control in robotic manipulation scenarios with an average of 2dB PSNR improvement over state-of-the-art video VAEs.

</details>


### [117] [How Should Vision-Language-Action Models Use Proprioceptive State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03052)
*Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Chen,Ziyang Rao,Pengteng Li,He Zhang,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统回答了VLA模型中本体感受状态输入的三个关键问题：当前状态是否改善闭环控制、状态历史有多大帮助及其作用机制、状态应在哪一层（视觉语言骨干还是动作生成模块）输入，并提出了可测试的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型普遍将机器人本体感受状态作为输入，但集成方式各异（文本提示、视觉语言前缀、动作专家直连），且几乎只用单帧。这导致三个悬而未决的问题：当前状态是否真的提升闭环控制、状态历史的价值来源、状态应该在哪一层输入。

Method: 在流匹配VLA上进行受控实验，固定骨干网络、训练数据、动作表示和评估协议。实现五种代表性接口（离散状态提示、VLM前缀、动作前缀、状态专家、特征调制），在45个原子任务（覆盖三个任务族）和20个组合任务上评估，并将状态历史长度从1帧扫描到96帧。

Result: 实验对三个问题给出了系统性答案，提炼出可测试的状态感知VLA设计原则。

Conclusion: 通过严格的消融对比，为VLA中状态输入的接口选择、历史长度和注入层次提供了经验性设计指导。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models almost universally take robot proprioceptive state as input, yet wire it in incompatible ways -- serialized into text prompts, projected into the vision-language prefix, or fed directly to the action expert -- and almost always as a single current frame. Three questions remain open: (1) whether, and on which tasks, current state actually improves closed-loop control; (2) how much state history helps, and whether its benefit reflects genuine temporal variation rather than added conditioning capacity; and (3) where state should enter the model -- the vision-language backbone or the action-generation module. We answer these questions through controlled experiments on a flow-matching VLA, fixing the backbone, training data, action representation, and evaluation protocol throughout. We implement five representative interfaces -- discrete state prompt, VLM prefix, action prefix, state expert, and feature modulation -- under matched implementation details, and evaluate them on 45 atomic tasks spanning three task families plus 20 composite tasks; we then sweep the state-history length from 1 to 96 frames to examine how historical state information affects model performance. The experiments yield systematic answers to all three questions, distilled into testable design principles for state-aware VLAs.

</details>


### [118] [Passively Safe Convex Guidance for Cislunar Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03060)
*Ian M. Down,Connor Plaks,Matthew Bolliger,Michael Caudill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出用于地月空间被动安全脉冲交会与近距离操作的纯凸优化方案，涵盖接近、到达和中止机动，并在机动执行误差和导航不确定性下验证，服务于NASA CAPSTONE 02任务的天体制导程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间（特别是南方9:2同步近直线晕轨道）中的自主交会与近距离操作需要机载可高效求解的安全制导方法，但现有的非凸或启发式方法难以兼顾实时性与安全性保证。

Method: 将交会与接近操作的机动规划建模为纯凸优化问题，设计被动安全的接近、到达和中止策略，结合机动执行误差和导航不确定性进行鲁棒约束，并集成到用于自主机载执行的制导框架中。

Result: 通过使用规划的相对导航传感器套件和测量节奏的高保真闭环蒙特卡洛仿真，验证了机动设计性能，确认满足任务需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸优化制导方法构成NASA CAPSTONE 02任务的基线机载制导例程，具备实际任务部署前景，并在仿真中证实其安全性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents purely convex programs for passively safe impulsive rendezvous and proximity operations in cislunar orbits. Approach, arrival, and abort maneuvers are all designed and validated in the context of maneuver execution error and navigation uncertainty, and formulated for efficient onboard execution in the autonomous scenario. The outlined methods form the baseline onboard guidance routines for NASA's CAPSTONE 02 mission planned to demonstrate autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations capabilities in the southern 9:2 synodic near rectilinear halo orbit. High fidelity closed loop Monte Carlo simulations using the planned relative navigation sensor suite and measurement cadence verify the intended maneuver design performance.

</details>


### [119] [A Hierarchical Approach to Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Requiring Time Varying Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03103)
*Rishabh Shukla,Adithya Santhosh,Shaili Gandhi,Samrudh Moode,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DPA-FTG框架，将扩散策略的低频规划（5 Hz）与基于力的高频阻抗控制器（60 Hz）解耦，解决接触丰富拆解任务中扩散策略推理延迟与开环执行盲区之间的权衡问题，在双臂电池拆解任务中超越Reactive Diffusion Policy等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在复杂多模态机器人操作中表现优异，但在接触丰富的拆解任务中受限：迭代去噪过程带来推理延迟，难以支撑高频动态交互（如凿击、撬动）；而动作分块技术虽降低延迟但使用开环执行窗口，对断裂事件引起的快速力瞬变无感知。

Method: DPA-FTG采用双层架构：高层（5 Hz）使用条件扩散模型预测潜在参数序列，从学习到的任务原语词汇表中选择策略；低层（60 Hz）使用轻量级力条件策略作为神经阻抗控制器，实时调节执行以维持接触稳定性。该设计将低频规划与高频力调节解耦，区别于仅关注位置校正的视觉-触觉方法。

Result: 在双臂电池拆解任务（涉及柔顺薄片分离）上，DPA-FTG优于包括Reactive Diffusion Policy在内的最先进基线。

Conclusion: 将扩散策略的高层规划与基于力的高频反馈控制解耦，是解决接触丰富拆解任务中延迟-闭环失衡问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown strong performance in learning complex, multi-modal behaviors for robotic manipulation. However, their application to contact-rich disassembly tasks remains limited by a key trade-off: the iterative denoising process introduces inference latencies that makes high frequency control difficult, which is essential for realizing dynamic interactions such as chiseling and prying. Recent action-chunking techniques mitigate latency but use an open-loop execution window, rendering the system blind to rapid force transients caused by fracture events. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Diffusion Policy Augmented by Fast Trajectory Generation (DPA-FTG). Compared to recent visual-tactile approaches that focus on positional correction, DPA-FTG decouples low-frequency planning from high-frequency force regulation. At the high level ($5$ Hz), a conditional diffusion model predicts a sequence of latent parameters for selecting a strategy from a learned vocabulary of task primitives. At the low level ($60$ Hz), a lightweight, force-conditioned policy acts as a neural impedance controller, modulating execution in real-time to maintain contact stability. We validate our approach on a bimanual battery disassembly task involving the separation of a compliant sheet. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that DPA-FTG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP).

</details>


### [120] [Shooting for Contact: Contact-Implicit Multiple Shooting for Dynamic Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03116)
*Sergio A. Esteban,Jason H. K. Siu,Derrick Mach,Junheng Li,Vince Kurtz,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可微分模拟器的接触隐式直接模拟多段打靶（DSMS）框架，将运动学可行的参考轨迹转化为动态可行的全身运动轨迹，加速运动模仿RL训练并实现Unitree G1上的零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法过度强调运动学相似性，忽略全身动力学、接触一致性和执行器限制，导致生成的参考轨迹难以被RL策略复现，尤其在接触丰富的行为中。

Method: 将可微分模拟器嵌入非线性规划（NLP）中，采用直接模拟多段打靶方法，在求解过程中内隐地解决接触、摩擦、碰撞、自碰撞和关节限位等问题，无需预设接触时间表或显式接触约束，同时施加跟踪、执行器和任务约束。

Result: 与现有重定向方法相比，DSMS加速了运动模仿RL训练，产生高成功率和低跟踪误差的策略；在Unitree G1上实现了命令条件化的接触丰富爬行和180度跳转的零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: DSMS通过接触隐式的可微分模拟优化，有效弥合了运动学参考与动态可行轨迹之间的鸿沟，显著改善RL策略学习和真实世界部署性能。

Abstract: Motion retargeting approaches often prioritize kinematic similarity over whole-body dynamics, contact consistency, and actuation limits, yielding references that are difficult for reinforcement learning (RL) policies to reproduce, particularly for contact-rich behaviors. We present a contact-implicit, direct simulation-based multiple shooting (DSMS) framework that transforms kinematically feasible references into dynamically feasible whole-body trajectories. By embedding a differentiable simulator within a nonlinear program, DSMS resolves contact, friction, impacts, self-collision, and joint limits internally while enforcing tracking, actuation, and task constraints without prescribing a contact schedule or introducing explicit contact constraints. Compared with existing retargeting methods, DSMS accelerates motion-imitation RL training and yields policies with high success rates and low tracking error. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on the Unitree G1 through command-conditioned contact-rich crawling and a highly dynamic 180-degree jump-turn.

</details>


### [121] [POMDPs for Autonomous Science Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03155)
*Daniel Guirguis,Nathan Wallace,Hanna Kurniawati,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP)，通过在高维观测空间中对推演的物理属性进行分支而非原始传感器数据，使科学驱动的信念空间规划变得可行。该方法在50维观测的扩展RockSample域上较连续观测基线提升18.6%奖励并减少32.9%计算时间，在Cuprite高光谱地质勘探中信息增益比最优信息论基线高2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索任务需要在传感器不确定性和计算约束下做决策，但将科学表征纳入POMDP规划因高维观测空间而难以实现。现有信息论规划器假设确定性观测，牺牲了POMDP提供的原理性不确定性量化。需要一个框架在保持完整传感器信息的同时，使科学驱动的信念空间规划在计算上可行。

Method: SHM-POMDP引入科学假设图层（分层概率模型），规划器在推断的物理属性（科学假设/地图要素）层面上进行分支，而非直接在原始高维传感器数据上分支。通过学习得到的观测模型保留完整传感器信息，使规划器能联合推理导航与科学属性，在不确定性下做决策，并通过维持信念和自适应重规划来最大化信息增益。

Result: 在50维观测的扩展RockSample域上，SHM-POMDP比连续观测基线获得18.6%更高的奖励、减少32.9%的每步计算时间。在Cuprite高光谱地质勘探中，较最优信息论基线获得2.5倍更高的信息增益，仅用均匀先验即可达到oracle性能的80%。

Conclusion: 将分层概率模型集成到信念空间规划中，能够实现可处理的、有原则的自主科学探索，在性能上同时超越传统POMDP方法和科学感知的信息论方法，证明了科学假设层级抽象是解决高维观测信念规划的关键路径。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration missions require decision-making under sensor uncertainty and computational constraints, yet integrating scientific representations into POMDP planning has remained intractable due to high-dimensional observation spaces. Information-theoretic planners overcome this by assuming deterministic observations, sacrificing the principled uncertainty quantification that POMDPs provide. We introduce the Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP), which makes science-driven belief-space planning more tractable by branching on inferred physical properties rather than raw sensor data. This preserves full sensor information through learned observation models while enabling the planner to reason jointly about navigation and scientific properties under uncertainty. On an extended RockSample domain with 50-dimensional observations, SHM-POMDP achieves 18.6\% higher rewards and 32.9\% reduced computation time per step than continuous-observation baselines. On realistic geologic exploration using Cuprite hyperspectral data, SHM-POMDP achieves 2.5$\times$ higher information gain than the best information-theoretic baseline by maintaining beliefs and replanning adaptively---reaching 80\% of oracle performance using only uniform priors. These results demonstrate that integrating hierarchical probabilistic models into belief-space planning enables tractable, principled autonomous science that outperforms both traditional POMDP methods and science-aware information-theoretic approaches.

</details>


### [122] [Accelerating Human-Aware Robot Trajectory Generation via Diffusion and Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03159)
*Byeong-Il Ham,Hyun-Bin Kim,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向人机交互场景的机械臂约束运动规划框架：利用RRT/RRT*生成碰撞与自碰撞规避的训练数据，训练扩散模型进行约束满足轨迹生成，并通过一致性蒸馏加速推理，联合加权加加速度正则化提升轨迹平滑度。仿真显示150条候选轨迹生成时间小于100ms，且加加速度显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非冗余机械臂在完全指定末端位姿时，碰撞避免和自碰撞规避等额外约束难以作为简单的零空间次级任务处理，导致在同时考虑安全与运动学约束的HRI环境中难以生成可行的关节空间轨迹。

Method: 1) 使用RRT/RRT*算法生成碰撞与自碰撞感知的轨迹数据集；2) 训练扩散模型通过引导采样生成满足约束的轨迹；3) 应用一致性蒸馏减少迭代扩散采样的推理时间；4) 在损失函数中引入联合加权加加速度正则化项以惩罚关节加速度突变，提升轨迹平滑性。

Result: 仿真结果表明：一致性模型能在100ms内生成150条轨迹候选，保持高任务成功率，且在施加加加速度正则化后关节和末端执行器的加加速度显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束运动规划框架能在HRI环境中高效生成安全、平滑且约束满足的关节空间轨迹，兼顾了推理速度与轨迹质量。

Abstract: This research proposes a constrained motion planning framework for robot manipulators in human-robot interaction (HRI). For a non-redundant manipulator with a fully specified end-effector pose, additional requirements such as collision avoidance and self-collision avoidance are difficult to handle as simple null-space secondary tasks. This limitation makes it challenging to generate feasible joint-space trajectories in HRI environments where safety and kinematic constraints must be considered simultaneously. To address this limitation, collision- and self-collision-aware trajectories are generated using Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and RRT* algorithms, and the resulting dataset is used to train a diffusion model that generates constraint-satisfying trajectories through guided sampling. To reduce the inference time required for iterative diffusion sampling, consistency distillation is applied, and a joint-weighted jerk regularization term is incorporated into the loss function to promote smoother trajectories by penalizing abrupt changes in joint acceleration. Simulation results show that the consistency model generates 150 trajectory candidates in less than 100 ms, maintains a high episode success rate, and substantially reduces joint and end-effector jerk when jerk regularization is applied.

</details>


### [123] [PFM-HR: Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03227)
*Yukang Gao,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Xingyu Chen,Zhaorui Wang,Fanghai Zhang,Hanyang Cao,Zhengyang Shen,Ji Ma,Runhan Zhang,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PFM-HR是一种可复用的流匹配先验模型，直接在大规模无序姿态数据上训练，通过Pose Geometry Score (PGS)量化策略生成的关节坐标变化与先验姿态几何的契合度，并用PGS调制跟踪奖励来引导策略探索，从而提升物理仿真人形机器人运动跟踪的强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动先验用于人形机器人跟踪时存在局限：时间先验需要有序的运动片段，而姿态先验对策略引发的姿态转换提供的指导有限。作者希望利用大规模无序姿态数据训练可复用的先验，在不依赖有序数据的前提下更好地引导策略进行结构化姿态变化。

Method: 提出Pose Flow Matching (PFM-HR)：在无序姿态数据上训练的流匹配先验；提出Pose Geometry Score (PGS)量化rollout中关节坐标变化与先验捕获的姿态变化局部几何的一致性；利用PGS调制跟踪奖励来引导策略探索，先验在多个跟踪任务间保持冻结。

Result: 实验表明PFM-HR在单一运动跟踪和通用运动跟踪任务上都取得了改进，尤其在高动态运动上提升明显。

Conclusion: PFM-HR通过可复用的无序姿态先验配合PGS引导奖励调制，有效提升了物理仿真人形机器人的运动跟踪强化学习效果，特别是高动态场景。

Abstract: Motion priors improve reinforcement learning for physics-based humanoid tracking, but temporal priors require ordered motion clips, while pose priors provide limited guidance for policy-induced pose transitions. We present Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots (PFM-HR), a reusable flow matching prior trained directly on large scale unordered pose data. PFM-HR introduces the Pose Geometry Score (PGS), which quantifies how joint coordinate changes during rollouts align with the local geometry of pose variation captured by the prior. Using PGS to modulate the tracking reward guides policy exploration toward structured pose changes while keeping the prior frozen across tracking tasks. Experiments demonstrate that PFM-HR improves both single motion and general motion tracking, especially for highly dynamic motions.

</details>


### [124] [Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning: Defending Vision-Language-Action Robots Against Physical Attention Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03231)
*Jinquan Zhang,Dongfu Yin,Run Yang,Yufeng Yan,Zhen Tian,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示VLA策略易受物理可实现的对抗补丁攻击，提出一种"注意力劫持"机制及攻击方法AGSD，并引入零推理开销的防御SARF，在LIBERO仿真与PiPER真实机器人上显著降低失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略虽然在机器人操作中前景广阔，但其对物理世界攻击的鲁棒性极弱。作者发现通过可打印的对抗补丁即可可靠地诱发失败，其根因是"策略关键的动作-视觉注意力劫持"机制——动作条件化注意力从任务相关区域被转移到局部补丁上。理解并防御这一机制级脆弱性是保障VLA机器人安全部署的关键。

Method: 攻击端提出AGSD：基于EOT优化的可打印补丁，联合实现(i)将动作-视觉注意力集中到补丁上和(ii)破坏视觉-语言语义对齐，具备跨任务与跨架构迁移能力。防御端提出SARF：仅微调视觉编码器，利用特征锚定、策略关键注意力修正、语言引导的几何一致性（且仅限制在语义相关区域）实现零推理开销防护。

Result: 在LIBERO上，SARF将OpenVLA在AGSD攻击下的失败率从100%降至14.2%-56.8%（平均28.6%），同时保持干净性能；在真实PiPER机械臂上，AGSD攻击下平均成功率从23.0%提升至65.0%。攻击展现强跨任务与跨架构迁移性，防御在仿真与真实机器人上均有效。

Conclusion: 机制级鲁棒性是保障VLA机器人在物理注意力劫持攻击下安全性的实用路径。理解注意力层面的脆弱性比单纯叠加防御更有效，SARF通过轻量级视觉编码器微调即可实现高效防护。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies promise general robotic manipulation, but their robustness against physical-world attacks remains fragile. In particular, we show that physically realizable adversarial patches can reliably induce failures by triggering a mechanism we call policy-critical action-to-vision attention hijacking, where action-conditioned attention is diverted from task-relevant regions to a localized patch. To demonstrate the threat, we propose Attention-Guided Semantic Disruption (AGSD), an Expectation-over-Transformation (EOT) optimized printable patch that jointly (i) concentrates action-to-vision attention on the patch and (ii) disrupts vision-language semantic alignment, yielding strong cross-task and cross-architecture transfer. To mitigate such attacks, we introduce Structure-Aware Robust Fine-Tuning (SARF), a zero-inference-overhead defense that fine-tunes only the visual encoder using feature anchoring, policy-critical attention correction, and language-guided geometric consistency restricted to semantically relevant regions. On LIBERO, SARF reduces OpenVLA's failure rate under AGSD from 100% to 14.2%-56.8% (28.6% average) across suites while preserving clean performance, and on a real PiPER manipulator it improves average success under AGSD from 23.0% to 65.0%. These results highlight mechanism-level robustness as a practical path to securing VLA robots against physical attention hijacking.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Context-Aware Motion Priors for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03234)
*Yunyang Mo,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Hanyang Cao,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知运动先验（CMP）框架，通过高优势策略 rollout 学习上下文-运动兼容性得分，并结合演示目标保持相关性，使运动先验能根据当前任务上下文自适应地重加权参考监督，用于训练轻量级上下文条件适配器，从而为类人形策略学习提供更相关的运动引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验方法通常从头到尾在整个参考数据集上学习一个通用的、与任务无关的先验，并在策略训练中统一应用，无法区分哪些参考运动与当前任务上下文相关，可能提供不相关甚至冲突的引导。

Method: CMP 使用高优势策略 rollout 学习上下文-运动兼容性，同时用基于演示的目标确保所学相关性仍然植根于参考分布；相关性分数用于对参考监督进行重加权，训练一个轻量级的上下文条件适配器。CMP 可与 Adversarial Motion Priors 和 Score-Matching Motion Priors 两种基础先验方法实例化。

Result: 在五个类人形控制任务上，CMP 一致地提升了任务性能和样本效率，学习了有意义的上下文-运动对齐，且对不平衡的参考分布具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将运动先验自适应地适配到任务上下文可以为类人形策略学习提供更相关的引导，CMP 是一种通用、无需人工技能标签或单独技能发现阶段的框架。

Abstract: Motion priors provide powerful guidance for learning naturalistic humanoid behaviors. However, existing methods typically learn a general, task-agnostic prior from the entire reference dataset and apply it uniformly throughout policy training. As a result, the prior cannot distinguish which reference motions are relevant to the current task context, potentially providing irrelevant or conflicting guidance. We present Context-Aware Motion Priors (CMP), a framework that adapts a general motion prior to the current task context without manual skill labels, dataset partitioning, or a separate skill discovery stage. Specifically, CMP learns context-motion compatibility using high-advantage policy rollouts, while a demonstration-based objective keeps the learned relevance grounded in the reference distribution. The resulting relevance scores reweight reference supervision for training a lightweight context-conditioned adapter. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of CMP, we instantiate it with both Adversarial Motion Priors and Score-Matching Motion Priors. Across five humanoid control tasks, CMP consistently improves task performance and sample efficiency, learns meaningful context-motion alignment, and remains robust to imbalanced reference distributions. These results show that adapting motion priors to task contexts provides more relevant guidance for humanoid policy learning.

</details>


### [126] [GraspMeanFlow: SE(3)-Equivariant MeanFlow for Few-Step 6-DoF Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03295)
*Jiyong Kwon,Yikun Bai,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GraspMeanFlow，一个SE(3)-等变的均值流框架，用于少步6-DoF抓取位姿生成。通过学习有限时间区间内的平均速度（通过时间有序指数定义，精确再现刚体位姿累积位移），实现单次或少量函数评估即可生成高质量抓取位姿，无需迭代数值积分。在ACRONYM数据集上验证，单次函数评估即可达到迭代模型需5步才能接近的EMD，且分布严格随物体旋转平移等变变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SE(3)-等变流模型虽然能生成与物体旋转平移一致变换的抓取位姿，但依赖迭代数值积分，每次抓取需要数十次函数评估，严重限制实时操作应用。需要一种既能保持等变性又能通过少步骤（甚至单步）采样生成高质量抓取的方法。

Method: 核心思想是学习有限时间区间的平均速度场，通过时间有序指数（time-ordered exponential）定义，使其精确再现该区间内的刚体位姿累积位移。方法包括：1）证明以点云为条件的分布经等变平均速度流映射传输后仍保持不变性，从而在少步采样下保留等变性；2）通过对时间对进行提升为等变向量的方式对场进行时间对条件化，主干网络不变；3）使用流匹配边界项搭配两种一致性项之一进行稳定训练：微分MeanFlow恒等式（需要Jacobian-向量积）或等价半群损失（避免该计算）。

Result: 在ACRONYM基准上实验表明：1）GraspMeanFlow单次函数评估即可达到迭代SE(3)流模型需5步才接近的EMD；2）同一框架的第二种实例化在少步机制下抓取成功率提升最高24.3个百分点；3）两种实例化生成的抓取分布都严格随物体旋转平移变换。

Conclusion: GraspMeanFlow通过均值流框架解决了等变流模型采样速度慢的瓶颈，在单步或少步采样下同时保持SE(3)等变性和高抓取质量，为实时6-DoF抓取生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent data-driven methods for synthesizing 6-DoF grasp poses use generative models to learn complex grasp pose distributions and generate diverse candidate poses. In particular, SE(3)-equivariant flow-based models generate grasp poses that transform consistently with object rotations and translations. However, these methods sample by iterative numerical integration, requiring tens of function evaluations per grasp and limiting their use in real-time manipulation. We propose GraspMeanFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant MeanFlow framework for few-step 6-DoF grasp generation. Our method learns the average velocity over a finite time interval, defined through the time-ordered exponential so that it reproduces exactly the rigid-body displacement accumulated over that interval. We prove that a point-cloud-conditioned distribution transported by an equivariant average-velocity flow map remains invariant, so equivariance is retained under few-step sampling, and we condition the field on a pair of times by lifting both to equivariant vectors, leaving the backbone otherwise unchanged. For stable training, we pair a flow-matching boundary term with either of two consistency terms: the differential MeanFlow identity, whose target requires a Jacobian-vector product, or an equivalent semigroup loss that avoids it. Experiments on ACRONYM show that a single function evaluation of GraspMeanFlow reaches the EMD that an iterative SE(3) flow model needs five steps to approach, that a second instantiation of the same framework improves grasp success by up to 24.3 points in the few-step regime, and that both generate grasp distributions transforming exactly with the object.

</details>


### [127] [PLS-Calib: A Partial Least Squares Framework for Event Camera and Odometry Calibration under Ground Motion Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03296)
*Guangyu Li,Xiao Li,Yujie Wu,Changshuo Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Jiang Cai,Fangwen Yu,Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLS-Calib，首次将偏最小二乘（PLS）回归应用于非同步异构传感器流之间的旋转外参标定，解决了地面机器人运动受限条件下传统CCA方法协方差矩阵病态导致的数值不稳定问题，并在事件相机与里程计标定任务中引入了极性感知事件表示以增强圆形标定靶的时空对比度，最终以闭式稳定解实现了对标定鲁棒性和精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器旋转外参标定方法依赖6-DoF运动激励全部自由度，但地面受限机器人无法满足此条件；CCA-based方法虽然适配受限设置，却因协方差矩阵病态导致数值不稳定和标定精度下降。因此需要一种在受限运动条件下仍能稳定、精确估计旋转外参的方法。

Method: 1）首次将PLS回归引入旋转外参标定，建模异步异构传感器流之间的潜在运动学相关性；2）提出极性感知事件表示（polarity-aware event representation），增强圆形标定靶在事件相机中的时空对比度；3）PLS闭式解天然避免CCA中的矩阵奇异问题，提供数值稳定的标定结果。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上验证了方法有效性，相比现有SOTA方法在标定鲁棒性和精度方面均有显著提升，且无需6-DoF运动激励。

Conclusion: PLS-Calib为受限机器人系统提供了一种实用且理论扎实的旋转标定方案，并开辟了统计学习方法在神经形态视觉中应用的新方向。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic rotation calibration between sensors is fundamental to the performance of robotic perception systems. However, most existing calibration techniques rely on full 6-DoF motion to excite all degrees of freedom, which is often infeasible for ground-constrained robots with limited motion capabilities. Recent approaches designed for such restricted settings, such as Canonical Correlation Analysis (CCA)-based methods, suffer from ill-conditioned covariance matrices that lead to numerical instability and suboptimal calibration accuracy. To overcome these limitations, we present a novel rotation calibration framework named PLS-Calib that, for the first time, leverages Partial Least Squares (PLS) regression to model the latent kinematic correlations between asynchronous, heterogeneous sensor streams. Specifically, we apply our method to the calibration of an event camera and an odometry onboard a ground robot. To improve event-based pattern detection, we introduce a polarity-aware event representation, which enhances spatiotemporal contrast in circular calibration targets. Our PLS-based formulation yields a closed-form, stable solution that avoids matrix singularities inherent in CCA-based approaches. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating significant improvements in calibration robustness and accuracy over state-of-the-art methods. This work offers a practical and theoretically grounded solution for rotation calibration in constrained robotic systems and opens up new directions for applying statistical learning techniques in neuromorphic vision.

</details>


### [128] [Shaping Wind-Tunnel Airflow for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles using Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03378)
*Ghadeer Elmkaiel,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线学习算法，用于控制多风扇垂直风洞中的复杂气流场。该方法将简化物理模型与基于迭代测量的学习相结合，实现样本高效的收敛至目标气流分布，并能生成均匀、高斯、抛物线和被动滑翔等复杂气流剖面，显著提升滑翔机器人的飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进的空中机器人开发和测试需要可控环境中的定制气流剖面，但多风扇风洞中气流场的精确控制极具挑战性。

Method: 结合简化物理模型与迭代测量驱动的在线学习算法，利用实测数据不断更新模型，实现样本高效地收敛到期望气流分布。算法支持可变数量的风扇，并对目标气流剖面（均匀、高斯、抛物线及面向被动滑翔的气流）进行自适应控制。

Result: 成功生成多种复杂气流剖面（均匀、高斯、抛物线），并生成专门设计的被动滑翔气流剖面，大幅提升滑翔机器人的飞行性能；算法在不同风扇数量下均表现出良好的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线学习控制方法能高效、灵活地控制多风扇风洞的复杂气流场，为空中机器人实验环境提供可靠的气流生成工具，尤其在被动滑翔任务中展现出显著的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The development and testing of advanced aerial robots require experiments in controlled environments with tailored airflow profiles. This paper presents an online learning algorithm for controlling the complex airflow field in a multi-fan vertical wind tunnel. Our method combines a simplified physical model with iterative, measurement-based learning, enabling sample-efficient convergence to desired airflow distributions. We demonstrate the method's versatility by generating complex airflow, such as uniform, Gaussian, and parabolic profiles. Crucially, we show that our algorithm can produce an airflow profile specifically designed for passive soaring, greatly enhancing flight performance of a soaring robot. Variability, practical utility, and robustness of our approach are further highlighted by successful operation with a varying number of fans.

</details>


### [129] [RoboReact: Agentic Skill Distillation from Generated Egocentric Videos for Generalizable Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03387)
*Shuliang He,Shuai Wang,Bo Yue,Junchi Teng,Changyu Wang,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboReact是一个从单目RGB-D观察自动合成全身人形机器人操作技能的框架。它通过视频生成模型合成人类操作视频，利用深度感知3D重建提取几何保持的交互关键帧，再重定向到高自由度人形平台，并通过在线目标中心重定向和VLM引导的优化循环来弥合想象计划与物理执行之间的差距，最终通过全身控制器执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要灵巧操作能力，但多样化和可泛化的技能获取成本高昂，因为硬件数据采集昂贵且标注劳动密集。虽然视频生成模型能合成丰富的操作经验，但将这些想象行为转化为可执行的人形全身技能仍是空白。

Method: 1) 从单个RGB-D观察生成人类操作视频；2) 通过深度感知3D重建提取保持几何的交互关键帧；3) 将这些关键帧重定向到高自由度人形平台并保持手-物交互几何；4) 在线目标中心重定向（object-centric re-grounding）弥合规划与执行差距；5) 利用视觉语言模型引导的优化循环适应几何失配和执行偏差；6) 通过全身控制器执行精细化技能。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上的实验表明，RoboReact能跨多样物体配置泛化，并在不依赖遥操作或人工演示的情况下，从执行扰动中稳健恢复。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了将生成模型、视觉语言推理和闭环控制相结合，可实现可扩展的人形技能获取，为合成数据驱动的人形机器人学习提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the potential to perform dexterous manipulation in human environments, yet acquiring diverse and generalizable skills remains costly due to expensive hardware data collection and labor-intensive annotation. Recent advances in video generative models provide a promising opportunity to synthesize rich manipulation experiences from visual observations, but transferring such imagined behaviors into executable whole-body humanoid skills remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present RoboReact, a framework that automatically synthesizes whole-body humanoid manipulation skills from a single egocentric RGB-D observation. RoboReact generates human manipulation videos, extracts geometry-preserving interaction keyframes through depth-aware 3D reconstruction, and retargets them to high-DoF humanoid platforms while preserving hand-object interaction geometry. To bridge the gap between imagined plans and physical execution, RoboReact performs online object-centric re-grounding and leverages a vision-language model-guided refinement loop to adapt skills under geometric mismatch and execution deviations. The refined skills are executed through a whole-body controller, enabling coordinated whole-body manipulation and dexterous interaction. Experiments on real humanoid robots demonstrate that RoboReact generalizes across diverse object configurations and robustly recovers from execution disturbances without requiring teleoperation or human demonstrations. These results highlight the potential of combining generative models, vision-language reasoning, and closed-loop control for scalable humanoid skill acquisition.

</details>


### [130] [Flying over The Uncertain Nature (FORTUNE): Intelligent and Humanistic 3D Path Planning for Low-Altitude Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03408)
*Minghui Liwang,Wenhan Jia,Xinlei Yi,Wenbo Zhu,Yuhan Su,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FORTUNE，一个用于3D多无人机路径规划与任务分配的分层离线-在线框架，在不确定地面PoI需求下同时处理异质时空需求、环境不确定性和以人为中心的运行约束，并通过真实交通数据验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空智能体激增带来对环境感知的实时且社会负责的协同感知需求，但现有工作假设PoI静态且完全已知，无法处理异质时空需求、环境不确定性和以人为中心的运行约束三者并存的难题。

Method: 提出FORTUNE分层框架：离线阶段用Transformer预测Type-II PoI激活窗口，用增强麻雀搜索算法（结合优先级感知解码和危险感知进化）生成协同飞行计划；在线阶段用轻量级细化模块适应新出现的Type-III PoI同时保持全局任务一致性，建模泊松式持续需求、时间可预测需求和突发需求于统一框架，并加入依赖高度的噪声暴露和公共安全风险成本。

Result: 在真实交通数据和合成场景上的实验表明，FORTUNE在有效性、可扩展性和实际适用性上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FORTUNE通过分层离线-在线设计有效解决了大规模混合整数非线性问题，在不确定动态环境下兼顾感知性能与社会合规性，为多无人机协同感知提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of low-altitude intelligent agents is increasing the demand for timely and socially responsible collaborative sensing in dynamic urban environments. However, jointly addressing heterogeneous spatiotemporal demands, environmental uncertainty, and human-centered operational constraints remains challenging. This paper studies 3D multi-UAV path planning and task assignment under uncertain ground PoI demands. Unlike existing work assuming static and fully known PoIs, we model persistent, temporally predictable, and emergent demands within a unified framework. We further incorporate altitude-dependent societal and environmental costs, including noise exposure and public safety risks, to balance sensing performance with socially compliant operations. To solve the resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear problem, we propose FORTUNE, a hierarchical offline-online framework. Offline, a Transformer predicts Type-II PoI activation windows, while an enhanced sparrow search algorithm generates coordinated flight plans through priority-aware decoding and danger-aware evolution. Online, a lightweight refinement module accommodates emerging Type-III PoIs while preserving global mission coherence. Experiments on real-world traffic data and synthetic scenarios show that FORTUNE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in effectiveness, scalability, and practical applicability.

</details>


### [131] [A Low-Cost Hybrid Reservoir Computing Model for Isolated Sign Language Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03444)
*Nitin Kumar Singh,Arie Rachmad Syulistyo,Yuichiro Tanaka,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于轻量级储层计算(RC)的手语识别方法：MediaPipe提取身体和手部关键点，经混合储层计算(HRC，结合DRC和双向RC)变换为高维动态表示，最终用岭回归分类。在WLASL100数据集上Top-1/Top-5/Top-10准确率为61.12%/86.05%/92.56%，训练时间仅数秒，适合边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习手语识别(SLR)在边缘设备上计算开销过高，部署受限，需一种低计算成本的轻量级SLR方案。

Method: MediaPipe提取全身和手部关键点→混合储层计算(HRC)架构（深度储层计算DRC+双向储层计算BRC）将输入变换为高维动态表示→岭回归将最终HRC状态映射到类别标签。

Result: 在WLASL100视频数据集上Top-1/Top-5/Top-10准确率分别为61.12%/86.05%/92.56%，与DL方法性能相当。训练时间从DL(如Bi-GRU)的较长时间骤降至数秒。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低计算成本实现与DL方法相当的SLR性能，证明了RC方法在边缘设备上手语识别的部署潜力。

Abstract: Sign language recognition (SLR) enhances communication between hearing and hearing-impaired individuals. Although deep learning (DL) has achieved promising performance in SLR, its high computational cost limits deployment on edge devices. To address this challenge, we propose a lightweight reservoir computing (RC)-based approach for SLR. In the proposed method, MediaPipe extracts body and hand keypoints to capture the spatial and temporal dynamics of gestures. These keypoints are then processed by a hybrid reservoir computing (HRC) architecture that combines deep reservoir computing (DRC) and bidirectional reservoir computing (BRC), transforming the input into a high-dimensional dynamic representation. A ridge regression model maps the final HRC state to class labels. This HRC-based SLR method achieved Top-1, Top-5, and Top-10 accuracies of 61.12%, 86.05%, and 92.56%, respectively, on the Word-Level American Sign Language 100 (WLASL100) video dataset, demonstrating competitive performance compared to deep learning-based approaches. Additionally, due to the lightweight nature of RC, the training time was drastically reduced to only a few seconds compared with DL-based methods such as Bi-GRU.This method offers low computational cost, showing its potential for deployment on edge devices.

</details>


### [132] [Continue or Replan? Bernoulli-Continuation Policy Learning for Adaptive Horizon Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03483)
*Weichen Xu,Zhenhua Liu,Lin Luo,Yaobo Liang,Chengtang Yao,Qingyu Mei,Jian Cao,Xixin Cao,Xing Zhang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP)，一种轻量级即插即用的自适应执行地平线框架，通过将固定长度动作块的地平线选择分解为逐段"继续或重新规划"决策，并以强化学习从轨迹级结果训练，联合奖励任务成功与VLA使用效率。在RoboTwin 2.0上平均成功率+11.08%（13个低成功率任务）和+4.06%（全部50个任务），并且无需再训练即泛化到Randomized设置和不同基座策略，在真实机器人上成功率从74%→92%与44%→84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分块(chunk)的VLA模型在执行固定数量的动作后才重新规划，重规划变成与任务进度无关的周期性调度。当关键操作阶段前没有重规划边界时，会基于过时的行动块执行关键操作，导致失败。作者旨在实现任务自适应的执行地平线，而非固定周期重规划。

Method: BCP在固定长度动作块上添加一个continuation head，将执行地平线选择分解为连续的"继续或重新规划"(continue-or-replan)二元决策，对候选地平线施加序数、前缀共享的归纳偏置。由于每个块的最优地平线不可观测，使用强化学习从轨迹级结果训练，并设计Replanning-Efficiency Reward联合奖励任务成功与VLA使用效率，防止策略退化为过短地平线。基座VLA保持冻结，实现轻量即插即用。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0 + LingBot-VLA上：13个低成功率任务平均成功率+11.08%；全部50个任务从89.88%→93.94%(+4.06%)。仅用Clean设置训练就能泛化到Randomized设置(+4.06%)。迁移到不同基座策略π0.5，LIBERO上+1.7%，LIBERO-PRO上+6.8%。真实机器人两个操作任务成功率从74%→92%和44%→84%。由于开销极小且成功率更高，整体运行时间反而低于固定地平线基线。

Conclusion: BCP通过将执行地平线选择建模为序数的逐段决策并用RL训练，实现了任务自适应的重规划调度，无需修改基座VLA即可显著提升任务成功率，并展现出良好的跨设置、跨基座策略和跨任务迁移能力，同时运行开销可忽略。

Abstract: Existing chunk-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models execute a fixed number of actions (i.e., execution horizon) before replanning, turning replanning into a task-agnostic periodic schedule that is independent of task progress. As a result, when no replanning boundary falls before a critical manipulation stage, it is executed from a stale chunk rather than a freshly replanned one. To address this limitation, we propose Bernoulli-Continuation Policy (BCP), a lightweight, plug-and-play framework for adaptive horizon execution that keeps the base VLA frozen. Given a fixed-length action chunk, its continuation head decomposes execution-horizon selection into a sequence of continue-or-replan decisions, which imposes an ordinal, prefix-sharing inductive bias over candidate horizons rather than treating them as independent classes. Since the optimal horizon for each chunk is not observable, we train this head with reinforcement learning from trajectory-level outcomes and introduce a Replanning-Efficiency Reward that jointly rewards task success and efficient VLA usage, discouraging the policy from collapsing to unnecessarily short horizons. On RoboTwin 2.0 with LingBot-VLA as the base policy, BCP improves the average success rate by +11.08% on 13 low-success tasks and from 89.88% to 93.94% (+4.06%) across all 50 tasks. Although trained only under the Clean setting, BCP generalizes to the Randomized setting, raising the average success rate by +4.06%. It also transfers to a different base policy $π_{0.5}$, achieving a better result on LIBERO (+1.7%) and, notably, on the harder LIBERO-PRO (+6.8%). On a real robot, BCP lifts success from 74% to 92% and from 44% to 84% on two manipulation tasks. Meanwhile, its negligible overhead, combined with higher success, makes BCP's overall runtime even lower than the fixed-horizon baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Lightweight 3D Object Detection via Mamba-Based Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03490)
*Quoc Cuong Ninh,Huy Xuan Pham,Anh Tung Nguyen,Dinh Hoan Trinh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于知识蒸馏的轻量级LiDAR 3D目标检测框架，利用Mamba（选择性状态空间模型）构建多分支教师骨干网络，并通过体素空间特征对齐机制将物体级体素表征从强教师模型迁移到轻量级学生模型，在降低计算开销的同时保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR 3D检测方法依赖复杂架构和大量上下文信息提升精度，导致计算成本高，在资源受限的嵌入式设备（如自动驾驶车载平台）上性能不佳。需要一种在精度与效率之间取得更好平衡的方案。

Method: 提出知识蒸馏框架：(1) 设计基于Mamba的多分支教师骨干网络，利用选择性状态空间的线性时间序列建模能力；(2) 提出box-aware特征迁移机制，通过Mamba投影模块在体素空间中对教师和学生网络的对应体素特征进行选择性对齐；(3) 将物体级体素表征从教师蒸馏至轻量级学生模型。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实世界数据上的实验表明，该方法显著降低了计算负载，同时与最先进方法相比保持了竞争性的检测精度。

Conclusion: Mamba-based知识蒸馏是LiDAR 3D检测在嵌入式平台上实现精度-效率平衡的有效途径，验证了选择性状态空间模型在特征蒸馏和体素级特征对齐中的适用性。

Abstract: 3D object detection using light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensors requires a balance between accuracy and computational efficiency for onboard perception in autonomous driving and robotic navigation. Many existing LiDAR-based detection methods employ complex architectures to extract features, integrating large amounts of contextual information to enhance accuracy. This often results in significant computational costs, leading to suboptimal performance on resource-constrained embedded devices. In this study, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers object-level voxel representations from a strong teacher model to lightweight student models through selective voxel-space feature alignment. Taking advantage of the linear-time sequence model with selective state spaces (Mamba), we design a multi-branch Mamba teacher backbone and a box-aware feature transfer mechanism that aligns spatially corresponding voxel features between teacher and student networks through a Mamba-based projection module. Experimental results on both a public dataset and real-world data show that our approach significantly reduces computational load while maintaining competitive accuracy compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [134] [Principles of Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03496)
*Daniele Gammelli,Joseph Lorenzetti,Katie Luo,Gioele Zardini,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本书是斯坦福大学数十年教学积累的机器人自主系统教材，以统一概念框架整合经典机器人与现代物理AI，覆盖现代自主系统堆栈的核心方法，并配套Jupyter notebooks和实践练习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主机器人在道路、空中、仓库和太空中的广泛应用，机器人自主性已从学术研究演变为成熟工程实践，需要一本能衔接经典机器人与现代物理AI的统一教材。

Method: 以单一概念框架统摄现代自主系统堆栈的核心要素，每个主要主题配以Jupyter notebooks和实现驱动的练习，使读者在建立理论理解的同时获得实践直觉。

Result: 形成了一本原理性、可理解且面向部署的综合性教材，适合学生、工程师和研究人员进入这一快速增长领域。

Conclusion: 该书为设计、分析和贡献下一代自主系统提供了原则性且可访问的基础，是进入自主机器人领域的全面资源。

Abstract: Autonomous robots are moving rapidly from research labs into everyday life - on roads, in the air, in warehouses, and in space. Robot autonomy is no longer solely an academic pursuit, but a collection of mature, field-tested methods and tools that practitioners rely on in real-world deployments. This book offers a clear, unified introduction to the methods that make this possible. Built on decades of teaching at Stanford, the text develops the core elements of modern autonomy stacks within a single conceptual framework, bridging classical robotics and modern physical AI. Every major topic is paired with hands-on Jupyter notebooks and implementation-driven exercises, so readers build practical intuition alongside theoretical understanding. The result is a principled, accessible, and deployment-aware foundation for anyone seeking to design, analyze, or contribute to the next generation of autonomous systems. This is a comprehensive resource for students, engineers, and researchers entering one of today's fastest-growing fields.

</details>


### [135] [Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction: Bridging Long Horizons via Dynamical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03521)
*Xiucong Zhao,Jindong Tian,Hao Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PCTP（Pivot-Centric Trajectory Prediction），通过引入"枢轴点"将长期轨迹预测分解为短期子任务，将预测过程解耦为枢轴预测和基于枢轴的轨迹细化两个阶段，在Argoverse I和Argoverse II上提升了多种SOTA模型的预测精度。
